Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the boltzmann object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 417. The moduli space has dimension at most 836 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on boltzmann structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 417. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 836. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on boltzmann structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 51. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 975 with relations in degree 3.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
